across rows 21-38 and numeric data in year columns.\nseries_col = None\nseries_row_start = 21\nseries_row_end = 38\n\n# Find which column has a known series code at row 21.\nprobe_code = ws_data.cell(row=r, column=c).value == probe_code:\n                found = (r, c)\n                break\n        if found:\n            break\n    if not found:\n        raise RuntimeError(f'Could series_row_start + 17)\n\n# Define ranges for INDEX/MATCH\n# Values range is the year columns across the series-code rows.\nvalues_range = abs_range('Data', year_col_start, series_row_start, year_col_end, series_row_end)\nseries_range = abs_col_range('Data', series_col, series_row_start, series_row_end)\nheader_range = abs_row_range('Data', header_row, year_col_start,
